   185→        if (tag) {
   186→            return (
   187→                <XView className={styles.priceTag}>
   188→                    <XImageBackground
   189→                        source={{ uri: payTag }}
   190→                        resizeMode='stretch'
   191→                        imageStyle={zUtil.convertStyle({ width: 55, height: 23 })}
   192→                        style={specialStyle}
   193→                    >
   194→                        <XView className={styles.textContainer}>
   195→                            <XView className={styles.tagStyle}>{tag}</XView>
   196→                        </XView>
   197→                    </XImageBackground>
   198→                </XView>
   199→            );
   200→        }
   201→
   202→        return null;
   203→    };
   204→
   205→    const ButtonDescPop = ({ text, bgColor, buttonColor }) => {
   206→        return (
   207→            <XView className={classnames(styles.normalButtonDescWrapper)}>
   208→                <XView
   209→                    style={{ backgroundColor: bgColor }}
   210→                    className={classnames(styles.normalButtonDesc)}
   211→                >
   212→                    <XText className={styles.normalButtonDescText}>{text}</XText>
   213→                </XView>
   214→                <XView
   215→                    className={styles.descBottomView}
   216→                    style={{ backgroundColor: bgColor }}
   217→                >
   218→                    <XView
   219→                        className={styles.descCoverView}
   220→                        style={{ backgroundColor: buttonColor }}
   221→                    />
   222→                </XView>
   223→            </XView>
   224→        );
   225→    };
   226→
   227→    const getTraceInfo = (btnName: string) => {
   228→        return {
   229→            ...getCommonTripInfo(trainInfo, smartInfo, fromPage),
   230→            PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   231→            XProductList: priceDetailInfo?.priceDetailList?.map((item) => item.name),
   232→            Price: priceDetailInfo?.ticketPrice,
   233→            Type: getBtnSceneType(doubleButtonInfo),
   234→            ButtonName: btnName,
   235→            Vid: extInfo?.vid || smartInfo?.trainInfo?.vid || '',
   236→            TicketLeft: getHasLeftTicket() ? '1' : '0',
   237→            Tag: refundNotice || '',
   238→            TrainNumber: trainInfo?.trainNo || '',
   239→        };
   240→    };
   241→
   242→    const normalPay = zUtil.throttle(async (btnName: string) => {
   243→        if (isShowDetail) {
   244→            setShowDetail(false);
   245→        }
   246→        if (isResign && !resignInfo?.hasChangedFromStation) {
   247→            resignCreatOrder();
   248→
   249→            return;
   250→        }
   251→        const selectedPasList = chosenPasList.filter(item => item?.selected);
   252→        if (isResign && resignInfo?.hasChangedFromStation && selectedPasList.length > 0 && selectedPasList.every(item => item?.passengerType === '儿童票')) {
   253→            showConfirmDialog({
   254→                title: '温馨提示',
   255→                content: `当前订单含有儿童票，儿童不能单独出行，<font color="#198cff">请先选择一名成年乘客</font>`,
   256→                mainBtn: {
   257→                    text: '确认',
   258→                    callback: () => {
   259→                        xUbt.logTrace('TZARescheduleWrite_ChildForbiddenAlone_Yes_click', {
   260→                            PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   261→                        });
   262→                    }
   263→                },
   264→            })
   265→            xUbt.logTrace('TZARescheduleWrite_ChildForbiddenAlone_exposure', {
   266→                PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   267→            });
   268→
   269→            return;
   270→        }
   271→        if (isResign && resignInfo?.hasChangedFromStation && selectedPasList.length === 0) {
   272→            xShowToast({
   273→                title: '请先选择乘客',
   274→                duration: 2000,
   275→            });
   276→
   277→            return;
   278→        }
   279→        if (!resignInfo?.hasChangedFromStation) {
   280→            const checkoutIntercept = await dispatch(
   281→                checkBeforeOrder({
   282→                    updateT6Account: (value: Model.T6UserType) => commonDispatch(commonAction.updateAccount12306(value)),
   283→                }),
   284→            );
   285→            console.log('checkoutIntercept', checkoutIntercept?.payload, '下单前置校验结果');
   286→            xUbt.logDevTrace('check_before_order_result', {
   287→                result: checkoutIntercept?.payload ? '拦截' : '正常下单',
   288→                PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   289→            });
   290→            if (checkoutIntercept?.payload) {
   291→                xUbt.logTrace('TZMBookPage_Reserve_click', {
   292→                    ...getTraceInfo(btnName),
   293→                    OrderNumber: '',
   294→                });
   295→                return;
   296→            }
   297→        }
   298→
   299→        const bookParams: IBookUserInfo = {
   300→            updateT6Account: (value: Model.T6UserType) => commonDispatch(commonAction.updateAccount12306(value)),
   301→            traceInfo: getTraceInfo(btnName),
   302→        };
   303→        dispatch(createOrderAsync(bookParams));
   304→    }, 1000);
   305→
   306→    const crossPay = () => {
   307→        if (isCanNotUseCrossPay) {
   308→            return;
   309→        }
   310→        console.log('点击跨站下单');
   311→        dispatch(allProductActions.selectProductInfo({ code: 'doubleButtonInfo', isSelected: true }));
   312→        normalPay(doubleButtonInfo?.info?.withProductButton?.name || '');
   313→    };
   314→
   315→    const speedGrabBook = () => {
   316→        console.log('点击多专家抢票');
   317→        dispatch(allProductActions.selectProductInfo({ code: 'doubleButtonInfo', isSelected: true }));
   318→        normalPay(doubleButtonInfo?.info?.withProductButton?.name || '');
   319→    };
   320→
   321→    const resignCreatOrder = () => {
   322→        console.log('点击改签下单');
   323→        if (isShowDetail) {
   324→            setShowDetail(false);
   325→        }
   326→        if (choseResignPasCount <= 0) {
   327→            xShowToast({ title: '请先选择乘客～' });
   328→
   329→            return;
   330→        }
   331→        dispatch(fetchCreateResignOrder({ traceInfo: getTraceInfo('改签下单') }));
   332→    };
   333→
   334→    const trainsferFastPay = () => {
   335→        console.log('点击中转快速预定下单');
   336→        dispatch(allProductActions.selectProductInfo({ code: 'doubleButtonInfo', isSelected: true }));
   337→        normalPay(doubleButtonInfo?.info?.withProductButton?.name || '');
   338→    };
   339→
   340→
   341→    // 单程默认单按钮
   342→    const defaultPayButton = useMemo(() => {
   343→        return (
   344→            <>
   345→                <XView
   346→                    className={classnames(styles.buttonView, zUa.isMini() && styles.miniButtonView)}
   347→                    onClick={() => normalPay(isResign ? '改签' : '立即预订')}
   348→                >
   349→                    <XView
   350→                        className={styles.buttonText}
   351→                        style={zUtil.convertStyle({ fontWeight: 'bold' })}
   352→                    >
   353→                        {isResign ? `提交改签${choseResignPasCount}人` : '立即预订'}
   354→                    </XView>
   355→
   356→                    {!!refundNotice && (
   357→                        <ButtonDescPop
   358→                            text={refundNotice}
   359→                            bgColor={'#00C777'}
   360→                            buttonColor={'#198cff'}
   361→                        />
   362→                    )}
   363→                </XView>
   364→            </>
   365→        );
   366→    }, [choseResignPasCount, refundNotice]);
   367→
   368→    // 跨站省心预订双按钮
   369→    const trainCrossDoubleButton = useMemo(() => {
   370→        const { ordinaryButton, withProductButton } = doubleButtonInfo?.info || {};
   371→        return (
   372→            <XView className={styles.doubleButton}>
   373→                <XView
   374→                    className={styles.leftButton}
   375→                    onClick={() => normalPay(ordinaryButton?.name || '')}
   376→                >
   377→                    <XView className={styles.leftTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.name}</XView>
   378→                    <XView className={styles.leftSubTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.desc}</XView>
   379→                </XView>
   380→                <XView
   381→                    className={classnames(styles.rightButton, isCanNotUseCrossPay && styles.grayColor)}
   382→                    onClick={() => crossPay()}
   383→                >
   384→                    <XView className={classnames(styles.rightTitle, isCanNotUseCrossPay && styles.grayText)}>{withProductButton?.name}</XView>
   385→                    <XView className={classnames(styles.rightSubTitle, isCanNotUseCrossPay && styles.grayText)}>{withProductButton?.desc}</XView>
   386→                </XView>
   387→                <PriceTag
   388→                    tag={withProductButton?.tag}
   389→                    specialStyle={zUtil.convertStyle({ marginTop: -30, marginRight: zUa.isApp() ? -8 : 0 })}
   390→                />
   391→            </XView>
   392→        );
   393→    }, [isCanNotUseCrossPay, doubleButtonInfo?.info]);
   394→
   395→    function clickDetail() {
   396→        if (chosePasCount <= 0) {
   397→            xShowToast({ title: '请先选择乘客～' });
   398→
   399→            return;
   400→        }
   401→        console.log('点击明细', isShowDetail);
   402→        setShowDetail(!isShowDetail);
   403→    }
   404→
   405→    const doubleBtnSkeleton = () => {
   406→        return (
   407→            <XView className={styles.doubleButtonSke}>

   409→                <XView className={classnames(styles.doubleButtonRightCont)}></XView>
   410→            </XView>
   411→        );
   412→    };
   413→
   414→    const smartBtnSkeleton = () => {
   415→        return (
   416→            <XView className={styles.singleButtonWithDetail}>
   417→                <XView className={classnames(styles.leftWhiteCont)}></XView>
   418→                <XView className={classnames(styles.rightWhiteCont)}></XView>
   419→            </XView>
   420→        );
   421→    };
   422→
   423→    // 智慧左右带明细的单按钮
   424→    const singleButtonWithDetail = useMemo(() => {
   425→        return (
   426→            <XView className={styles.singleButtonWithDetail}>
   427→                <XView className={styles.priceTagInfo}>
   428→                    <XView className={styles.priceInfo}>
   429→                        <XView
   430→                            className={styles.priceSymble}
   431→                            style={zStyleUtils.zxRegular}
   432→                        >
   433→                            <XView className={styles.priceSymbleText}>
   434→                                {!!(hasStudent || hasChild) && '约'}
   435→                            </XView>
   436→                            ¥
   437→                        </XView>
   438→                        <XView
   439→                            className={styles.priceTitle}
   440→                            style={zStyleUtils.zxRegular}
   441→                        >
   442→                            {calculatedPrice}
   443→                        </XView>
   444→                    </XView>
   445→                    {/* 选中积分 */}
   446→                    {isPointSelected && priceDetailInfo?.pointDeductionAmount ? (
   447→                        <XText className={styles.tag}>
   448→                            {`12306积分已抵¥${priceDetailInfo.pointDeductionAmount}`}
   449→                        </XText>
   450→                    ) : null}
   451→                    {/* 选中优惠 */}
   452→                    {priceDetailInfo?.discountPrice ? (
   453→                        <XText className={styles.tag}>
   454→                            {`已优惠¥${priceDetailInfo.discountPrice}`}
   455→                        </XText>
   456→                    ) : null}
   457→                </XView>
   458→                <XView className={styles.flexRowStart}>
   459→                    <XView
   460→                        className={styles.flexRowStart}
   461→                        onClick={() => clickDetail()}
   462→                    >
   463→                        <XView className={styles.detail}>明细</XView>
   464→                        {isShowDetail ? <IconDownArrow /> : <IconUpArrow />}
   465→                    </XView>
   466→                    <XView
   467→                        className={styles.buttonInfo}
   468→                        onClick={() => normalPay('下一步')}
   469→                    >
   470→                        <XText
   471→                            className={styles.buttonTitle}
   472→                            style={{ ...zStyleUtils.bold, fontFamily: 'PingFangSC-Semibold' }}
   473→                        >
   474→                            立即预订
   475→                        </XText>
   476→                        {!!refundNotice && (
   477→                            <XViewExposure
   478→                                ref={() => {
   479→                                    xExposure.refreshExposure(getOrderBookPage());
   480→                                }}
   481→                                testID={{
   482→                                    ubtKey: 'TZMBookPage_FreeReturn_exposure',
   483→                                    data: { PageId: getOrderBookPageId() },
   484→                                    pageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   485→                                }}
   486→                            >
   487→                                <XText className={styles.buttonDesc}>{refundNotice}</XText>
   488→                            </XViewExposure>
   489→                        )}
   490→                    </XView>
   491→                </XView>
   492→            </XView>
   493→        );
   494→    }, [isShowDetail, priceStr, refundNotice, isPointSelected, priceDetailInfo?.pointDeductionAmount, calculatedPrice, hasStudent, hasChild, priceDetailInfo?.totalPrice]);
   495→
   496→    const transferPureDoubleButtion = useMemo(() => {
   497→        const { ordinaryButton, withProductButton } = doubleButtonInfo?.info || {};
   498→
   499→        return (
   500→            <XView className={styles.doubleButton}>
   501→                <XView
   502→                    className={classnames(styles.leftButton)}
   503→                    onClick={() => normalPay(ordinaryButton?.name || '')}
   504→                >
   505→                    <XView className={styles.leftTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.name || ''}</XView>
   506→                    <ZRichText className={styles.leftSubTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.desc?.[0] || ''}</ZRichText>
   507→                </XView>
   508→                <XView
   509→                    className={classnames(styles.rightButton)}
   510→                    onClick={() => trainsferFastPay()}
   511→                >
   512→                    <XView className={styles.rightTitle}>{withProductButton?.name || ''}</XView>
   513→                    <ZRichText className={styles.rightSubTitle}>{withProductButton?.desc?.[0] || ''}</ZRichText>
   514→                </XView>
   515→            </XView>
   516→        );
   517→    }, [doubleButtonInfo]);
   518→
   519→    const getBottomRemarkView = useMemo(() => {
   520→        if (remarkDescList?.length > 0) {
   521→            return (
   522→                <XView className={classnames(styles.remarkView)}>
   523→                    {remarkDescList.map((desc) => (
   524→                        <XView
   525→                            key={desc}
   526→                            className={classnames(styles.flexRowStart, styles.alignStart)}
   527→                        >
   528→                            {remarkDescList?.length > 1 && <XView className={styles.cycleTag} />}
   529→                            <ZRichText className={styles.remarkDesc}>{desc}</ZRichText>
   530→                        </XView>
   531→                    ))}
   532→                </XView>
   533→            );
   534→        }
   535→        return <LowPriceCombineSeatView />;
   536→    }, [remarkDescList]);
   537→
   538→    // 判断国庆大促banner是否应该展示
   539→    const shouldShowNationalDayBanner = useMemo(() => {
   540→        const { leftIcon } = activityInfo?.info || {};
   541→        return !!(leftIcon && chosenPasList?.length > 0 && !isPointSelected);
   542→    }, [activityInfo?.info, chosenPasList, isPointSelected]);
   543→
   544→    // 国庆大促banner
   545→    const nationalDayBannerView = useMemo(() => {
   546→        const { leftIcon, rightText, unselectCouponRightText } = activityInfo?.info || {};
   547→        const selectedCouponCode = selectCoupon?.selectInfo?.couponCode || '';
   548→
   549→        let rightShowText = '';
   550→        let isSelected = false;
   551→        if (selectedCouponCode) {
   552→            isSelected = true;
   553→            const selectedCouponBannerText = selectCoupon?.info?.couponDetailInfo?.find((item) => item?.couponCode === selectedCouponCode)?.selectedBannerText || '';
   554→            rightShowText = selectedCouponBannerText;
   555→        } else {
   556→            isSelected = false;
   557→            rightShowText = unselectCouponRightText || '';
   558→        }
   559→        if (shouldShowNationalDayBanner) {
   560→            return (
   561→                <XViewExposure className={styles.nationalDayBannerView} ref={() => xExposure.refreshExposure(getOrderBookPage())} testID={{
   562→                    ubtKey: 'TZMBookPage_ActivityBanner_exposure',
   563→                    data: {
   564→                        ActivityType: "国庆专享礼",
   565→                        Type: rightShowText || '',
   566→                        PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   567→                    },
   568→                    pageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   569→                }}>
   570→                    <XView className={styles.nationalDayBannerCenterView}>
   571→                        <XImage className={styles.nationalDayBannerImg} mode="heightFix" src={leftIcon || ''}></XImage>
   572→                        <XView className={styles.nationalDayBannerRight}>
   573→                            {
   574→                                isSelected && (<XImage className={styles.iconImg} src='https://dimg04.c-ctrip.com/images/2330h12000nmy5nkb9289.png'></XImage>)
   575→                            }
   576→                            <ZRichText className={styles.nationalDayBannerRightText}>{rightShowText}</ZRichText>
   577→                            {
   578→                                rightText && rightShowText && (
   579→                                    <XView className={styles.splitLine}></XView>
   580→                                )
   581→                            }
   582→                            <ZRichText className={styles.nationalDayBannerRightText}>{rightText || ''}</ZRichText>
   583→                        </XView>
   584→                    </XView>
   585→                </XViewExposure>
   586→            );
   587→        }
   588→        return null;
   589→    }, [activityInfo?.info, selectCoupon?.info?.couponDetailInfo, couponBottomInfo, shouldShowNationalDayBanner]);
   590→
   591→    const byteDanceBannerView = useMemo(() => {
   592→        const { leftIcon, activityCode } = dyPayInfo?.info || {};
   593→        const isSelected = !!dyPayInfo?.selectInfo?.isSelected;
   594→        if (!leftIcon || isPointSelected || activityCode !== ActivityCodeEnum.DY_PAY || chosenPasList?.length <= 0) {
   595→            return null;
   596→        }
   597→        const handleToggleSelection = () => {
   598→            dispatch(selectProdWithMutexAsync({
   599→                code: 'dyPayInfo',
   600→                selectInfo: {
   601→                    isSelected: !isSelected,
   602→                },
   603→            }));
   604→        };
   605→        return <ByteDanceBanner info={dyPayInfo?.info || {}} isSelected={isSelected} page={getOrderBookPage()} pageId={getOrderBookPageId()} onToggleSelection={handleToggleSelection} />;
   606→    }, [dyPayInfo, chosenPasList, isPointSelected]);
   607→
   608→    const useCouponInfoView = useMemo(() => {
   609→        // 活动类（如元旦春运大促）> 抖音 > 老客感知banner
   610→        // 活动类 和 抖音支付babanner 的互斥 后端处理，选优惠券后展示的banner是基于用户操作的，所以前端处理
   611→        if (!shouldShowNationalDayBanner && !zUtil.isEmptyObject(couponBottomInfo?.couponBannerInfo) && zUtil.isEmptyObject(dyPayInfo?.info)) {
   612→            return (
   613→                <XViewExposure
   614→                    testID={{
   615→                        ubtKey: 'TZMBookPage_DiscountCountdownBan_exposure',
   616→                        data: {
   617→                            PageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   618→                            CouponCode: selectCoupon?.selectInfo?.couponCode || '',
   619→                        },
   620→                        pageId: getOrderBookPageId(),
   621→                    }}
   622→                    className={styles.couponView}>
   623→                    <XView className={styles.titleView}>
   624→                        {!!couponBottomInfo?.couponBannerInfo?.icon && (
   625→                            <XImage
   626→                                src={couponBottomInfo?.couponBannerInfo?.icon}
   627→                                className={styles.couponIcon}
   628→                                mode="heightFix"
   629→                            />
   630→                        )}
   631→                        <ZRichText className={styles.couponTitle}>{couponBottomInfo?.couponBannerInfo?.title || ''}</ZRichText>
   632→                    </XView>
   633→                    <ZRichText className={styles.couponDesc}>{couponBottomInfo?.couponBannerInfo?.expirationTime || ''}</ZRichText>
   634→                </XViewExposure>
   635→            );
   636→        }
   637→        return null;
   638→    }, [dyPayInfo, selectCoupon?.selectInfo?.couponCode, shouldShowNationalDayBanner, couponBottomInfo]);
   639→
   640→    const grabBookingPureButton = useMemo(() => {
   641→        const { ordinaryButton, withProductButton } = doubleButtonInfo?.info || {};
   642→        return (
   643→            <XView className={styles.doubleButton}>
   644→                <XView
   645→                    className={styles.leftButton}
   646→                    onClick={() => normalPay(ordinaryButton?.name || '')}
   647→                >
   648→                    <ZRichText className={styles.leftTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.name || ''}</ZRichText>
   649→                    <ZRichText className={styles.leftSubTitle}>{ordinaryButton?.desc?.[0] || ''}</ZRichText>
   650→                </XView>
   651→                <XView
   652→                    className={styles.rightButton}
   653→                    onClick={() => speedGrabBook()}
   654→                >
   655→                    <ZRichText className={styles.rightTitle}>{withProductButton?.name || ''}</ZRichText>
   656→                    <ZRichText className={styles.rightSubTitle}>{withProductButton?.desc?.[0] || ''}</ZRichText>
   657→                </XView>
   658→            </XView>
   659→        );
   660→    }, [doubleButtonInfo]);
   661→
   662→    /* 
   663→      isShowMultBtnSkeleton：骨架屏的展示状态
   664→      一般调用完产品接口后会隐藏骨架屏
   665→      其他场景需要手动改为false，如盲盒、改签
   666→      请注意！！！
   667→    */
   668→    const payBarView = useMemo(() => {
   669→        if (getOrderBookPagePayButtonStyleABTestValue() === 'B' && !isMultiOrder && !isCrossOrder) {
   670→            if (mergeFillPageExtMap?.isBlockGroupPeople == 1) {
   671→                // 单按钮带明细
   672→                return singleButtonWithDetail;
   673→            } else if (isShowMultBtnSkeleton) {
   674→                return smartBtnSkeleton();
   675→            }
   676→        }
   677→        if (isCrossOrder) {
   678→            // 后跨（可能会有省心预定产品）出骨架屏
   679→            if (isShowMultBtnSkeleton && isAfterCross) {
   680→                return doubleBtnSkeleton();
   681→            } else if (doubleButtonInfo?.info?.productType === DoubleButtonEnum.EASY_BOOKING) {
   682→                return trainCrossDoubleButton;
   683→            }
   684→        } else if (isMultiOrder) {
   685→
   686→            if (isShowMultBtnSkeleton) {
   687→                return smartBtnSkeleton();
   688→            } else if (
   689→                [DoubleButtonEnum.RELIEVED_TRAVEL_BG, DoubleButtonEnum.MANY_SPECIAL_PERSON_GRAB, DoubleButtonEnum.ALL_AROUND_GRAB].includes(
   690→                    doubleButtonInfo?.info?.productType,
   691→                )
   692→            ) {
   693→                const maskRule = doubleButtonInfo?.info?.withProductButton?.rule?.passengerMask;
   694→                if ((maskRule && !hasMaskPas) || !maskRule) {
   695→                    return grabBookingPureButton
   696→                }
   697→            } else if (doubleButtonInfo?.info?.productType === DoubleButtonEnum.TRANSFER_FAST_BUY) {
   698→                return transferPureDoubleButtion
   699→            }
   700→
   701→            return defaultPayButton;
   702→        }
   703→        return defaultPayButton;
   704→    }, [isCrossOrder, isMultiOrder, priceStr, isShowDetail, doubleButtonInfo?.info?.productType, hasMaskPas, choseResignPasCount, isShowMultBtnSkeleton, isAfterCross, priceDetailInfo?.pointDeductionAmount]);
   705→    const isChoose = (priceDetailInfo?.priceDetailList || []).some((item) => item?.productCancelFlag);
   706→
   707→    return (
   708→        <>
   709→            <XView className={styles.root}>
   710→                {getBottomRemarkView}
   711→                {useCouponInfoView}
   712→                {nationalDayBannerView}
   713→                {byteDanceBannerView}
   714→                <ZBottomDrawerBar>
   715→                    <ZBottomDrawerBar.Bar>

Now checking the design specification against what's actually implemented...

the Redux store is managing the dialog trigger signal, which is the right approach, while the actual dialog visibility stays in local component state. The `showFastTicketChannelFlag` gets read correctly from the polling response during the wait state. But I'm noticing the design expects a `BottomPayBanner.tsx` file that doesn't actually exist in the repository—it was supposed to be modified according to the spec, but only the `index.tsx` file at the root is present.

Checking against the review criteria, the core requirements are met: the modal's mask click is properly disabled, the dialog visibility is managed locally with useState, the fast ticket channel flag is being read in the polling callback, and there's idempotency protection in place.

The payment page navigation uses the correct order number from Redux state, the new components are memoized for performance, button colors match the design spec, and XButton is being used correctly. Now I'm checking the visibility logic more carefully—when the dialog flag becomes true and the dialog isn't currently shown, it should set the visibility state, which looks right. But I need to trace through how this interacts with the Redux state initialization.

The Redux flag only flips from false to true once due to the `hasShownFastTicketDialog` guard in the reducer, so the useEffect won't trigger again on subsequent polling cycles—only when the flag first becomes true. There's a potential edge case though: if the user closes the dialog and the Redux flag is already true, the condition checking `!isShowFastTicketDialog` would still be false, so the dialog wouldn't reopen. Let me verify this logic holds...

Actually, the condition `showFastTicketDialogFlag && !isShowFastTicketDialog` evaluates to `true && false = false`, so the dialog stays closed, which is correct. The implementation properly handles the idempotency requirement.

The Redux action's guard condition only sets the flag to true if it's not already been shown, so once the dialog appears, it can't trigger again. The `handleFastTicketPay` function correctly closes the dialog and navigates to payment with the order number. All the review criteria check out, and the implementation aligns with the design, so I'm ready to compile the quality report.
